    <script>
    function toggleMobileMenu() {
        var sb = document.querySelector('.sidebar');
        var bd = document.getElementById('sidebarBackdrop');
        sb.classList.toggle('open');
        bd.style.display = sb.classList.contains('open') ? 'block' : 'none';
    }
    // Close menu when nav item clicked on mobile
    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(function(item) {
        item.addEventListener('click', function() {
            if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
                var sb = document.querySelector('.sidebar');
                var bd = document.getElementById('sidebarBackdrop');
                sb.classList.remove('open');
                if (bd) bd.style.display = 'none';
            }
        });
    });
    </script>

    <script>
    (function(){
        const phrases = [
            "Calentando la plancha...",
            "Contando hamburguesas del s\u00e1bado...",
            "Pregunt\u00e1ndole a la IA cu\u00e1nta carne pedir...",
            "Midiendo el cierzo de Zaragoza...",
            "Revisando si el Arrabal ha abierto ya...",
            "Optimizando el turno de noche...",
            "Negociando con el proveedor de pan...",
            "Calculando cu\u00e1ntas patatas caben en la freidora...",
            "Buscando al junior que llega tarde...",
            "Conectando con Odoo a velocidad delivery...",
            "Comprobando si el responsable ha fichado...",
            "Analizando por qu\u00e9 el lunes siempre es flojo...",
            "Convenciendo al cierzo de que no sople hoy...",
            "Preparando los n\u00fameros como una buena smash...",
            "Verificando que el FIFO esta en orden...",
            "Poniendo la salsa secreta a los datos...",
            "Sumando tickets como si no hubiera ma\u00f1ana...",
            "Mirando si los Pilares caen en fin de semana...",
            "Haciendo inventario de servilletas... broma, de euros...",
            "Precalentando las previsiones a 180 grados...",
        ];
        let idx = Math.floor(Math.random() * phrases.length);
        const el = document.getElementById('loaderPhrase');
        if(el) el.textContent = phrases[idx];
        const iv = setInterval(() => {
            idx = (idx + 1) % phrases.length;
            if(el) { el.style.opacity = 0; setTimeout(() => { el.textContent = phrases[idx]; el.style.opacity = 1; }, 280); }
        }, 2500);
        window.addEventListener('load', () => {
            setTimeout(() => {
                clearInterval(iv);
                const loader = document.getElementById('appLoader');
                if(loader) { loader.style.opacity = '0'; setTimeout(() => loader.remove(), 600); }
            }, 600);
        });
    })();
    </script>

<script>
fetch('/api/pos-sync-status').then(r=>r.json()).then(d=>{
    if(d.status && d.status !== 'healthy'){
        document.getElementById('posSyncMsg').textContent = d.message || '';
        document.getElementById('posSyncBanner').style.display = 'block';
        if(d.status === 'stale' || d.status === 'no_data'){
            const b = document.getElementById('posSyncBanner');
            b.style.background = '#FEE2E2'; b.style.borderLeftColor = '#DC2626'; b.style.color = '#7F1D1D';
        }
    }
}).catch(()=>{});
</script>

function toggleExportMenu() {
    document.getElementById('exportMenu').classList.toggle('open');
}
function closeExportMenu() {
    document.getElementById('exportMenu').classList.remove('open');
}
document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
    if (!e.target.closest('.export-wrap')) closeExportMenu();
});

    <!-- INSIGHTS: Food cost trend + Stock health + Alertas + Export -->
    <div class="chart-container" id="insightsSection">
        <div class="chart-title" style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;">
            <span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;">

            <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;">
                <select id="insightsRange" onchange="loadInsights()" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:12px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;background:white;">
                    <option value="14">14 d</option>
                    <option value="30" selected="">30 d</option>

        <!-- Grid: 2 charts. IMPORTANTE: altura fija en el wrapper para que Chart.js (maintainAspectRatio:false) no crezca infinitamente. -->
        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:16px;">
            <div>
                <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--dark-slate);margin-bottom:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;">
                    <span>Evolución coste alimentos</span>
                    <span id="fcAvgPct" style="font-size:12px;background:#f1f5f9;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:100px;color:#475569;">media 0%</span>
                </div>
                <div style="position:relative;height:240px;max-height:240px;">
                    <canvas id="foodCostChart" style="max-height: 240px !important; display: block; box-sizing: border-box; height: 240px; width: 405px;" width="405" height="240"></canvas>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div>
                <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--dark-slate);margin-bottom:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;">
                    <span>Salud de stock</span>
                    <span id="stockSummary" style="font-size:12px;background:#f1f5f9;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:100px;color:#475569;">15 productos · 0 en 0 · 0 críticos</span>
                </div>
                <div style="position:relative;height:240px;max-height:240px;">
                    <canvas id="stockChart" style="max-height: 240px !important; display: block; box-sizing: border-box; height: 240px; width: 405px;" width="405" height="240"></canvas>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

    <script>
    window._foodCostChart = null;
    window._stockChart = null;

    function alertBadge(a) {
        const colors = {
            error: {bg:'#FEE2E2', border:'#DC2626', text:'#7F1D1D'},
            warn:  {bg:'#FEF9C3', border:'#D97706', text:'#854D0E'},
            info:  {bg:'#EFF6FF', border:'#2563eb', text:'#1E40AF'},
        };
        const c = colors[a.severity] || colors.info;
        const action = a.action ? ` <a href="${a.action}" style="color:${c.text};font-weight:700;text-decoration:underline;">Ver</a>` : '';
        return `<div style="padding:8px 14px;border-radius:8px;background:${c.bg};border-left:3px solid ${c.border};color:${c.text};font-size:13px;display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:10px;">
            <strong style="text-transform:uppercase;font-size:10px;letter-spacing:1px;flex-shrink:0;margin-top:2px;">${a.severity}</strong>
            <span style="flex:1;">${a.msg}${action}</span>
        </div>`;
    }

    async function loadInsights() {
        const days = parseInt(document.getElementById('insightsRange').value, 10) || 30;
        const locSel = document.getElementById('locationFilter');
        const locId = locSel && locSel.value ? '&location_id=' + encodeURIComponent(locSel.value) : '';
        try {
            const r = await fetch('/api/v3/insights?days=' + days + locId);
            const j = await r.json();

            // Alerts
            const alertsEl = document.getElementById('insightsAlerts');
            if (j.alerts && j.alerts.length) {
                alertsEl.innerHTML = j.alerts.map(alertBadge).join('');
            } else {
                alertsEl.innerHTML = `<div style="padding:10px 14px;border-radius:8px;background:#DCFCE7;border-left:3px solid #059669;color:#166534;font-size:13px;">Sin alertas. Operacion estable.</div>`;
            }

            // Food cost
            const fc = j.food_cost || {};
            const series = fc.series || [];
            document.getElementById('fcAvgPct').textContent = 'media ' + (fc.avg_pct || 0) + '%';
            if (window._foodCostChart) window._foodCostChart.destroy();
            const ctx1 = document.getElementById('foodCostChart').getContext('2d');
            window._foodCostChart = new Chart(ctx1, {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: series.map(s => s.date),
                    datasets: [{
                        label: '% food cost',
                        data: series.map(s => s.pct),
                        borderColor: '#f59e0b',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(245,158,11,0.15)',
                        fill: true,
                        tension: 0.3,
                        borderWidth: 2,
                        pointRadius: 2,
                    }, {
                        label: 'Umbral saludable (32%)',
                        data: series.map(() => 32),
                        borderColor: '#22c55e',
                        borderDash: [5,5],
                        pointRadius: 0,
                        borderWidth: 1.5,
                        fill: false,
                    }, {
                        label: 'Alarma (38%)',
                        data: series.map(() => 38),
                        borderColor: '#ef4444',
                        borderDash: [5,5],
                        pointRadius: 0,
                        borderWidth: 1.5,
                        fill: false,
                    }],
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    maintainAspectRatio: false,
                    plugins: {
                        legend: { position: 'bottom', labels: { boxWidth: 10, font: { size: 10 } } },
                    },
                    scales: {
                        y: { title: { display: true, text: '%' }, beginAtZero: true, max: 60 },
                    },
                },
            });

            // Stock health (horizontal bar: zero/critical/ok)
            const so = j.stockouts || {};
            const total = so.total_tracked || 0;
            const zero = so.at_zero || 0;
            const crit = so.critical || 0;
            const ok = Math.max(0, total - zero - crit);
            document.getElementById('stockSummary').textContent = total + ' productos · ' + zero + ' en 0 · ' + crit + ' críticos';
            if (window._stockChart) window._stockChart.destroy();
            const ctx2 = document.getElementById('stockChart').getContext('2d');
            window._stockChart = new Chart(ctx2, {
                type: 'bar',
                data: {
                    labels: ['En stock', 'Cr\u00edtico (<2)', 'Stockout (0)'],
                    datasets: [{
                        data: [ok, crit, zero],
                        backgroundColor: ['#22c55e', '#f59e0b', '#ef4444'],
                        borderRadius: 6,
                    }],
                },
                options: {
                    indexAxis: 'y',
                    responsive: true,
                    maintainAspectRatio: false,
                    plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
                    scales: { x: { beginAtZero: true } },
                },
            });
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('insights error', e);
        }
    }

    function exportWeeklyXlsx() {
        const locSel = document.getElementById('locationFilter');
        const qs = locSel && locSel.value ? '?location_id=' + encodeURIComponent(locSel.value) : '';
        window.location.href = '/api/export/weekly-report.xlsx' + qs;
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        loadInsights();
        // Re-load when location filter changes
        const locSel = document.getElementById('locationFilter');
        if (locSel) locSel.addEventListener('change', () => { loadInsights(); });
    });

    // ======== NOTIFICATIONS (Cookast AI) ========
    async function loadNotifications() {
        try {
            const r = await fetch('/api/v3/notifications');
            const j = await r.json();
            const box = document.getElementById('aiNotifications');
            const list = document.getElementById('aiNotifList');
            const meta = document.getElementById('aiNotifMeta');
            if (!box || !list) return;
            const notifs = j.notifications || [];
            if (!notifs.length) {
                box.style.display = 'block';
                meta.textContent = 'Todo estable — sin alertas ahora mismo.';
                list.innerHTML = `<div style="padding:10px 14px;border-radius:8px;background:rgba(34,197,94,0.08);border-left:3px solid #22c55e;color:#bbf7d0;font-size:13px;">Operación estable. Te avisaré si algo se desvía.</div>`;
                // Update sidebar badge
                setNotifBadge(0);
                return;
            }
            meta.textContent = `${j.critical} críticas · ${j.warning} avisos · ${j.info} info`;
            const sevColors = {
                critical: {bg:'rgba(239,68,68,0.12)', bd:'#ef4444', title:'#fca5a5', txt:'rgba(254,202,202,0.9)'},
                warning:  {bg:'rgba(245,158,11,0.12)', bd:'#f59e0b', title:'#fcd34d', txt:'rgba(253,230,138,0.9)'},
                info:     {bg:'rgba(34,211,238,0.1)',  bd:'#22D3EE', title:'#67e8f9', txt:'rgba(165,243,252,0.9)'},
            };
            list.innerHTML = notifs.map(n => {
                const c = sevColors[n.severity] || sevColors.info;
                const action = n.action_text ? `<a href="${n.action_url}" style="color:${c.title};font-weight:700;text-decoration:underline;font-size:12px;">${n.action_text} →</a>` : '';
                return `<div style="padding:10px 14px;border-radius:8px;background:${c.bg};border-left:3px solid ${c.bd};">
                    <div style="display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:10px;">
                        <span style="font-size:16px;flex-shrink:0;">${n.icon}</span>
                        <div style="flex:1;">
                            <div style="color:${c.title};font-weight:700;font-size:13px;margin-bottom:2px;">${n.title}</div>
                            <div style="color:${c.txt};font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.55;">${n.msg}</div>
                            ${action ? '<div style="margin-top:6px;">' + action + '</div>' : ''}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            }).join('');
            box.style.display = 'block';
            setNotifBadge(notifs.length);
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('notifications err', e);
        }
    }

    function setNotifBadge(count) {
        const aiNav = document.querySelector('a[href="#ai"], [data-page="ai"].nav-item');
        if (!aiNav) return;
        let badge = aiNav.querySelector('.notif-badge');
        if (count > 0) {
            if (!badge) {
                badge = document.createElement('span');
                badge.className = 'notif-badge';
                badge.style.cssText = 'background:#ef4444;color:white;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;padding:1px 6px;border-radius:100px;margin-left:6px;';
                aiNav.appendChild(badge);
            }
            badge.textContent = count;
        } else if (badge) {
            badge.remove();
        }
    }

    function refreshNotifications() { loadNotifications(); }

    // Load notifications on page load + refresh every 5 min
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        loadNotifications();
        setInterval(loadNotifications, 300000);
    });

    // ======== Data freshness badge ========
    async function loadDataFreshness() {
        try {
            const r = await fetch('/api/date-range');
            const j = await r.json();
            const badge = document.getElementById('dataFreshness');
            if (!badge || !j.max_date) return;
            const d = new Date(j.max_date + 'T00:00:00');
            const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
            const diffDays = Math.round((today - d) / 86400000);
            const pretty = d.toLocaleDateString('es-ES', {day:'numeric', month:'short'});
            let color = 'var(--text-muted)';
            let label = 'Datos hasta ' + pretty;
            if (diffDays > 2) {
                color = '#D97706';
                label = `⚠ POS atrasado ${diffDays} d · último ${pretty}`;
            } else if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 1) {
                color = '#059669';
                label = `● Datos al día (${pretty})`;
            }
            badge.textContent = label;
            badge.style.color = color;
            badge.title = `Histórico POS: ${j.pos_dates || 0} días · forecast: ${j.forecast_dates || 0} días`;
        } catch (e) {}
    }
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', loadDataFreshness);
    </script>

    
    <!-- Precision rolling: Previsto vs Real con MAPE (solo admin/implementer, no cliente) -->
    <div class="chart-container" id="accuracySection">
        <div class="chart-title" style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;">
            <span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;">
                Precisión del forecast <small style="color:var(--text-muted);font-weight:400;">rolling · previsto vs real día a día</small>
                <span class="v3-tip" data-meth="accuracy_series" title="Cómo se calcula (click)">?</span>
            </span>
            <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;">
                <select id="accuracyRange" onchange="loadAccuracyChart()" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:12px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;background:white;">
                    <option value="14">14 días</option>
                    <option value="30" selected="">30 días</option>
                    <option value="60">60 días</option>
                    <option value="90">90 días</option>
                </select>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div id="accuracyKpis" style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:8px 0 6px;font-size:12px;">
            <span id="accMape" style="background:#EFF6FF;color:#1E40AF;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:100px;font-weight:700;" title="Error medio absoluto porcentual. Cuánto se desvía la previsión del real en promedio.">Error medio: 50.8%</span>
            <span id="accWithin10" style="background:#DCFCE7;color:#166534;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:100px;font-weight:700;" title="Porcentaje de días en los que acertamos con menos de un 10% de desviación">Dentro ±10%: 1.7%</span>
            <span id="accWithin20" style="background:#FEF3C7;color:#92400e;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:100px;font-weight:700;" title="Porcentaje de días en los que acertamos con menos de un 20% de desviación">Dentro ±20%: 10.3%</span>
            <span id="accBias" style="background:#f1f5f9;color:#475569;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:100px;font-weight:700;" title="Sesgo: negativo = sub-predecimos (te llega más gente de la prevista). Positivo = sobre-predecimos.">Sesgo: -50.8%</span>
            <span id="accSamples" style="color:var(--text-muted);padding:4px 10px;">5464 muestras</span>
        </div>
        <div id="accuracyInterp" style="display: block; margin: 6px 0px 14px; padding: 10px 14px; border-radius: 8px; font-size: 12.5px; line-height: 1.55; background: rgb(254, 226, 226); border-left: 3px solid rgb(220, 38, 38); color: rgb(127, 29, 29);"><strong>Precisión insuficiente (error 50.8%).</strong> Estamos <strong>sub-prediciendo (real &gt; previsto)</strong> con bias -50.8%. Causas habituales: hueco de datos POS, cambios de carta recientes, o el forecast antiguo no tenía las señales nuevas cargadas. Se recalibra solo en 2-3 semanas. El chat IA te puede ayudar a diagnosticarlo.</div>
        <div style="position:relative;height:260px;max-height:260px;">
            <canvas id="accuracyChart" style="max-height: 260px !important; display: block; box-sizing: border-box; height: 260px; width: 826px;" width="826" height="260"></canvas>
        </div>
        <div id="accuracyEmpty" style="display:none;padding:28px;text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:13px;">
            Todavía no hay suficiente histórico «previsto vs real». La gráfica se empieza a rellenar cuando Cookast lleva &gt;14 días sincronizando tu POS.
        </div>
    </div>
    <script>
    window._accuracyChart = null;
    async function loadAccuracyChart() {
        const days = parseInt(document.getElementById('accuracyRange').value, 10) || 30;
        try {
            const r = await fetch('/api/accuracy-series?days=' + days);
            const j = await r.json();
            const series = j.series || [];
            const summary = j.summary || {};
            const emptyEl = document.getElementById('accuracyEmpty');
            const canvas = document.getElementById('accuracyChart');
            if (!series.length) {
                emptyEl.style.display = 'block';
                canvas.style.display = 'none';
                return;
            }
            emptyEl.style.display = 'none';
            canvas.style.display = 'block';

            // Update KPI badges
            const fmtPct = (v) => (v === undefined || v === null) ? '--' : (v + '%');
            document.getElementById('accMape').textContent = 'Error medio: ' + fmtPct(summary.mape);
            document.getElementById('accWithin10').textContent = 'Dentro ±10%: ' + fmtPct(summary.pct_within_10);
            document.getElementById('accWithin20').textContent = 'Dentro ±20%: ' + fmtPct(summary.pct_within_20);
            const bias = summary.bias;
            const biasTxt = bias === undefined || bias === null ? '--' : (bias > 0 ? '+' : '') + bias + '%';
            document.getElementById('accBias').textContent = 'Sesgo: ' + biasTxt;
            document.getElementById('accSamples').textContent = (summary.total || series.length) + ' muestras';

            // Interpretacion contextual
            const interp = document.getElementById('accuracyInterp');
            const mape = summary.mape;
            let verdict = null;
            if (mape === undefined || mape === null) {
                verdict = null;
            } else if (mape < 10) {
                verdict = {bg:'#DCFCE7', bd:'#059669', color:'#166534', txt:
                    `<strong>Precisión excelente (error ${mape}%).</strong> El motor acierta con margen muy bajo. Puedes confiar en las previsiones para cerrar pedidos y cuadrantes.`};
            } else if (mape < 15) {
                verdict = {bg:'#DCFCE7', bd:'#059669', color:'#166534', txt:
                    `<strong>Precisión buena (error ${mape}%).</strong> En rango operativo saludable. El motor está bien calibrado.`};
            } else if (mape < 25) {
                verdict = {bg:'#FEF9C3', bd:'#D97706', color:'#854D0E', txt:
                    `<strong>Precisión mejorable (error ${mape}%).</strong> Revisa si hay eventos locales no cargados o gaps de POS que afecten el aprendizaje.`};
            } else {
                const biasDir = bias > 0 ? 'sobre-prediciendo (previsto > real)' : 'sub-prediciendo (real > previsto)';
                verdict = {bg:'#FEE2E2', bd:'#DC2626', color:'#7F1D1D', txt:
                    `<strong>Precisión insuficiente (error ${mape}%).</strong> Estamos <strong>${biasDir}</strong> con bias ${biasTxt}. Causas habituales: hueco de datos POS, cambios de carta recientes, o el forecast antiguo no tenía ${(summary.total||0)>500 ? 'las señales nuevas cargadas. Se recalibra solo en 2-3 semanas' : 'suficiente historico'}. El chat IA te puede ayudar a diagnosticarlo.`};
            }
            if (verdict) {
                interp.style.display = 'block';
                interp.style.background = verdict.bg;
                interp.style.borderLeft = '3px solid ' + verdict.bd;
                interp.style.color = verdict.color;
                interp.innerHTML = verdict.txt;
            } else {
                interp.style.display = 'none';
            }

            const labels = series.map(s => s.date);
            const predicted = series.map(s => s.predicted);
            const actual = series.map(s => s.actual);
            const mapeArr = series.map(s => s.mape_pct);

            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
            if (window._accuracyChart) window._accuracyChart.destroy();
            window._accuracyChart = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: labels,
                    datasets: [
                        {
                            label: 'Previsi\u00f3n',
                            data: predicted,
                            borderColor: '#0891B2',
                            backgroundColor: 'rgba(6,182,212,0.1)',
                            tension: 0.25,
                            fill: false,
                            borderWidth: 2,
                            pointRadius: 2,
                            yAxisID: 'y',
                        },
                        {
                            label: 'Real',
                            data: actual,
                            borderColor: '#22c55e',
                            backgroundColor: 'rgba(34,197,94,0.1)',
                            tension: 0.25,
                            fill: false,
                            borderWidth: 2,
                            pointRadius: 2,
                            yAxisID: 'y',
                        },
                        {
                            label: '% desv. (MAPE)',
                            data: mapeArr,
                            borderColor: '#f59e0b',
                            backgroundColor: 'rgba(245,158,11,0.12)',
                            tension: 0.25,
                            fill: true,
                            borderWidth: 1.5,
                            pointRadius: 0,
                            yAxisID: 'y1',
                            borderDash: [4, 4],
                        },
                    ],
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    maintainAspectRatio: false,
                    interaction: { mode: 'index', intersect: false },
                    layout: { padding: { top: 10, bottom: 4, left: 4, right: 4 } },
                    scales: {
                        y: { title: { display: true, text: '\u20ac' }, beginAtZero: true },
                        y1: {
                            position: 'right',
                            title: { display: true, text: '% desv.' },
                            beginAtZero: true,
                            suggestedMax: Math.max(60, Math.ceil(Math.max(...mapeArr) / 10) * 10 + 10),
                            grid: { drawOnChartArea: false },
                        },
                    },
                    plugins: {
                        legend: { position: 'bottom', labels: { boxWidth: 10, font: { size: 11 } } },
                        tooltip: {
                            position: 'nearest',
                            boxPadding: 4,
                            padding: 8,
                            titleFont: { size: 11 },
                            bodyFont: { size: 11 },
                            callbacks: {
                                afterBody: (items) => {
                                    const i = items[0].dataIndex;
                                    const s = series[i];
                                    return 'Desviaci\u00f3n: ' + s.deviation_pct + '% - Muestras: ' + s.samples;
                                },
                            },
                        },
                    },
                },
            });
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('accuracy chart error', e);
        }
    }
    // Load when forecast page is visible (same trigger as main dashboard load)
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', loadAccuracyChart);
    </script>

    <!-- Auto-schedule publish card (admin/implementer) -->
    <div class="chart-container" id="autoScheduleCard" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0f172a,#0c1a2e);color:white;border:1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.3);">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px;margin-bottom:10px;">
            <div>
                <div style="font-size:10px;letter-spacing:1.2px;color:#22D3EE;font-weight:800;text-transform:uppercase;margin-bottom:4px;">Planificación automática</div>
                <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:800;color:white;">Publicar turnos 14d en Odoo Planning</div>
                <div style="font-size:12px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.55);margin-top:2px;">Cookast genera el cuadrante desde el forecast + vacaciones + niveles R/S/J y lo publica con 1 click. Cada empleado lo verá en su calendario Odoo.</div>
            </div>
            <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;">
                <button onclick="runAutoSchedule(true)" style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);color:white;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2);padding:8px 16px;border-radius:8px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;">Previsualizar</button>
                <button onclick="runAutoSchedule(false)" id="btnPublishSchedule" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#06B6D4,#0891B2);color:white;border:none;padding:10px 20px;border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;font-weight:800;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(6,182,212,0.3);">Publicar ahora</button>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div id="autoScheduleResult" style="margin-top:12px;font-size:12px;color:rgba(255,255,255,0.75);"></div>
        <div id="staffDriftAlert" style="display:none;margin-top:10px;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:8px;background:rgba(245,158,11,0.15);border-left:3px solid #f59e0b;color:#fde68a;font-size:12.5px;"></div>
    </div>
    <script>
    async function runAutoSchedule(dryRun) {
        const btn = document.getElementById('btnPublishSchedule');
        const out = document.getElementById('autoScheduleResult');
        btn.disabled = true;
        out.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#22D3EE;">${dryRun ? 'Calculando propuesta...' : 'Generando y publicando en Odoo...'}</span>`;
        try {
            const r = await fetch('/api/v3/auto-schedule', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Origin': location.origin, 'Referer': location.href},
                body: JSON.stringify({days: 14, replace_existing: true, dry_run: dryRun}),
            });
            const j = await r.json();
            if (!j.ok && !dryRun) {
                out.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#fca5a5;">Error: ${j.error || 'publicacion fallida'}</span>`;
            } else {
                const tot = j.totals || j.preview?.totals || {};
                const msg = dryRun
                    ? `<strong>Propuesta</strong>: ${tot.shifts_planned||0} slots · ${tot.hours||0}h · <strong>${(tot.cost||0).toLocaleString('es-ES')} €</strong> · ${tot.unstaffed_shifts||0} sin cubrir. Pulsa "Publicar ahora" para llevarlo a Odoo.`
                    : `✓ Publicados <strong>${j.published}</strong> slots en Odoo Planning · reemplazados ${j.replaced_previous||0} previos · coste total <strong>${(tot.cost||0).toLocaleString('es-ES')} €</strong>${j.failed ? ` · <span style="color:#fca5a5;">${j.failed} errores</span>` : ''}.`;
                out.innerHTML = msg;
                if ((j.preview_warnings||[]).length) {
                    out.innerHTML += `<div style="margin-top:6px;color:#fde68a;">⚠ ${j.preview_warnings.join(' · ')}</div>`;
                }
            }
        } catch (e) {
            out.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#fca5a5;">Error de red: ${e.message}</span>`;
        }
        btn.disabled = false;
    }

    // Staff drift detection: compara planning Odoo vs forecast actual
    async function checkStaffDrift() {
        try {
            const r = await fetch('/api/v3/staff-drift?days=14');
            const j = await r.json();
            const box = document.getElementById('staffDriftAlert');
            if (!box) return;
            if (!j.has_drift) {
                box.style.display = 'none';
                return;
            }
            const msg = `<strong>⚠ Detectada desviaci&oacute;n:</strong> ${j.summary || 'El forecast ha cambiado vs cuando publicaste el plan'}. ` +
                        (j.overstaffed_days ? `Hay ${j.overstaffed_days} d&iacute;as con m&aacute;s gente de la necesaria (ahorrar&iacute;as ${(j.potential_saving||0).toLocaleString('es-ES')} €). ` : '') +
                        (j.understaffed_days ? `Hay ${j.understaffed_days} d&iacute;as cortos de personal. ` : '') +
                        `<a href="#" onclick="runAutoSchedule(false);return false;" style="color:#fde68a;font-weight:800;text-decoration:underline;">Re-planificar ahora</a>`;
            box.innerHTML = msg;
            box.style.display = 'block';
        } catch (e) {}
    }
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', checkStaffDrift);
    // Auto-check cada 10 min
    setInterval(checkStaffDrift, 600000);
    </script>

window.METHODOLOGY = {

        title: "Revenue previsto",
        body: `
            <h4>Qu&eacute; es</h4>
            <p>Suma de facturaci&oacute;n prevista para todos los turnos del periodo que has seleccionado (hoy, esta semana, 7/14/30 d&iacute;as). Expresado en euros.</p>
            <h4>C&oacute;mo se calcula</h4>
            <p>El motor Cookast V4 parte del <strong>baseline hist&oacute;rico</strong> de tu POS (media del mismo d&iacute;a de la semana, mismo mes) y le aplica multiplicadores en cadena:</p>
            <div class="methodology-formula">forecast_dia = baseline_historico
  &times; trend_YoY        (crecimiento/decrecimiento a&ntilde;o sobre a&ntilde;o)
  &times; indice_dia       (lun/mar/... bajo tu propia media)
  &times; indice_mes       (estacionalidad mensual)
  &times; eventos          (festivos, puentes, partidos locales)

  &times; nomina           (+/- 48 h antes/despues de pagar)
  &times; escolar          (calendario lectivo Zaragoza)
  &times; reservas         (si tienes modulo reservas en Odoo)</div>
            <p>Cap global aplicado: <code>0.35x &ndash; 2.20x</code> para evitar extrapolaciones.</p>

function openMethodology(key) {
    const m = window.METHODOLOGY[key];
    if (!m) return;
    document.getElementById('methTag').textContent = m.tag || 'KPI';
    document.getElementById('methTitle').innerHTML = m.title;
    document.getElementById('methBody').innerHTML = m.body;
    document.getElementById('methodologyBackdrop').classList.add('open');
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
}

function closeMethodology() {
    document.getElementById('methodologyBackdrop').classList.remove('open');
    document.body.style.overflow = '';
}

// ESC to close
document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') closeMethodology();
});

// Delegate clicks on .v3-tip with data-meth attribute
document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const tip = e.target.closest('[data-meth]');
    if (tip) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        openMethodology(tip.dataset.meth);
    }
});
</script>

    </main>

    </style>

    <script>
    (async function loadTokenBar(){
        try {
            const r = await fetch('/api/token-usage');
            const d = await r.json();
            const count = document.getElementById('tokenCount');
            const fill = document.getElementById('tokenBarFill');
            if(count) count.textContent = `${d.used||0}/${d.limit||100}`;
            if(fill) {
                const pct = Math.min(100, d.pct||0);
                fill.style.width = pct + '%';
                if(pct > 95) fill.style.background = '#DC2626';
                else if(pct > 80) fill.style.background = '#D97706';
            }
        } catch(e){}
    })();
    </script>
